# Smart Chat Bar Roadmap

Last updated: 2026-07-14

Scope: the smart chat bar is the combined message input, slash command panel, context assembler, permission gateway, and task scheduler surface for the Windows desktop app.

Current run addendum 2026-07-14 web search detail payload fixture: Chat SSE tool timeline parsing now preserves local OpenAI Responses `web_search_call` detail metadata beyond the existing action-only shape. `sseParser.ts` carries bounded `query`, `web_search_results`, `search_context`, `user_location`, `domains`, `allowed_domains`, and `blocked_domains` through the existing built-in provider tool payload path, while recursively dropping `encrypted_content`. `verify-chat-sse-parser.mjs` adds a completed web-search detail fixture covering web-search naming, completed status mapping, search-context/location/domain/result evidence, and encrypted payload non-retention. This remains local parsing only: no provider connection, provider send, web search execution, result URL fetch, workspace mutation beyond this parser/test/docs slice, raw provider payload persistence, or network sync was added. Live provider parity, persisted built-in tool search, backend/provider taxonomy convergence, result artifact correlation, and provider-specific UX polish remain follow-up work. `npm run verify:chat-sse`, `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist`, `npm run verify:chatbar-status-summary`, and `npm run typecheck:node` cover this slice.

Current run addendum 2026-07-13 Gemini error taxonomy: Chat SSE provider observability now recognizes Google/Gemini-style structured error payloads. `sseParser.ts` detects Gemini errors from `error.status` and `error.details[].@type` evidence even on generic `event: error` frames, maps them to `google_gemini`, prefers statuses such as `RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED` as the analytics code, and marks `RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED`, `UNAVAILABLE`, `DEADLINE_EXCEEDED`, and `ABORTED` as retryable. `parseChatSseFrame` now throws `ChatSseError` with the normalized Gemini code/retryable flag, and `parseProviderErrorAnalyticsSseFrame` emits `Gemini stream error.` summaries while keeping raw provider metadata out of persisted analytics. This does not change live provider transport, backend dispatch, model selection, credentials, transcript persistence, tool execution, connector state, or layout. Live Gemini transport validation, backend provider parity, persisted timeline search, and unified backend plan/tool events remain follow-up work. `npm run verify:chat-sse`, `npm run verify:provider-error-analytics`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist` cover this slice.

The importer normalizes them to `.helm-chart.yaml` / `.kustomization.yaml`, reads only `MAX_KUBERNETES_PACKAGE_CONFIG_PREVIEW_BYTES`, caps dependency/resource/image/patch samples with `MAX_KUBERNETES_PACKAGE_CONFIG_ITEM_PREVIEW`, redacts secret-looking values, preserves `application/vnd.cncf.helm.chart+yaml` and `application/vnd.kubernetes.kustomization+yaml` MIME provenance, and keeps the explicit Attach/Route review boundary. Helm previews list chart name/version/api/app/type and bounded dependencies; Kustomize previews list bounded resources, images, patches, and namespace. At this boundary, no helm/kubectl/kustomize command, chart dependency build, template rendering, cluster connection, registry lookup, network call, or provider send was performed. `npm run verify:channel-adapters`, `npm run verify:channel-adapter-route-order`, `npm run verify:channel-adapter-runtime-fixtures`, `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist`, and `npm run typecheck:node` cover this Kubernetes package config input slice and runtime `Chart.yaml` / `kustomization.yaml` golden fixtures; live Helm dependency resolution, chart template rendering, Kustomize build, cluster dry-run validation, registry-backed image provenance, policy admission checks, and provider-backed Kubernetes diagnostics remain follow-up work.

Current run addendum 2026-07-09 runtime Windows-native golden fixtures: Channel adapter runtime fixture verification now imports temporary workspace `.msg`, `.lnk`, `.reg`, `.wprp`, and `.dmp` selected files through the transpiled main-process `importChannelContext` harness. The assertions cover Outlook MSG OLE/string previews, Windows Shell Link header/string previews, registry key/value/deletion-marker previews with secret redaction, WPRP profile/provider previews, and minidump stream metadata while preserving no-Outlook/no-Shell/no-registry-mutation/no-WPR/no-debugger safety boundaries. `npm run verify:channel-adapter-runtime-fixtures`, `npm run verify:channel-adapter-route-order`, `npm run verify:channel-adapters`, `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist`, and `npm run typecheck:node` cover this runtime Windows-native golden fixture slice; packaged Electron IPC fixture imports, full MSG/MAPI parsing, live shortcut resolution, registry hive inspection, WPR execution, and debugger-backed dump analysis remain follow-up work.

- Slash command registry and parser are centralized in `src/renderer/src/chatCommands.ts`.
- Commands execute locally and never call the gateway or external services.
- `/fix`, `/test`, `/commit`, `/compact`, and `/memory` are now registered as read-only local intent/context commands; `/skills` additionally opens Skills Square as a workflow selection bridge and can focus a specific skill or workflow template from command arguments.
- The composer shows a command panel as soon as the user types `/`.
- Command output is appended to the active thread as a normal user/assistant pair, so it is visible and persisted with the current chat snapshot.
- `/model <alias>` and `/agent <id-or-name>` resolve against the loaded model/agent catalogs and update the composer selector without calling the gateway.
- `/fork <intent>` keeps fork runtime-mode metadata, creates an isolated Git worktree under the desktop data directory, creates a local `agent_run` fork thread pointing at that worktree, persists source/worktree/branch/base metadata on the thread, then switches the UI to that thread while leaving an audit message in the source thread; `/fork queue <task1; task2>` parses a bounded task list, creates one isolated fork-backed `agent_run` thread per queued subtask, stores queue group/index/status metadata, accepts optional `@agent` prefixes for per-subtask dispatch assignment, requests Approval Center review before the queue can start, and reports waiting/ready/blocked queue state in the source thread and thread list; `/fork dispatch` finds approved ready queue threads for the current workspace, forwards selected agent/model metadata, honors per-thread agent assignments when present, starts each as an agent run in its isolated worktree, and persists running/completed/blocked queue state; `/fork schedule [limit N]` scans the current workspace for approved ready queue threads, orders them by queue group/index/update time, preserves per-thread agent assignments, and reuses the same dispatch path without bypassing queue-start approval; later chat/terminal/files actions derive their workspace from the active fork thread so they stay on the isolated worktree, and thread-menu merge-back/discard lifecycle requests enter Approval Center before approved merge-back or controlled cleanup execution. Approved cleanup now applies an explicit branch policy: merged branches are deleted safely with `git branch -d`, while unmerged discarded branches are archived under `drsai/archive/...`.
- `/plan`, `/goal`, `/review`, `/fix`, `/test`, `/commit`, and `/fork` set a first-class runtime mode visible in the composer until cleared.
- Fork threads are marked in the thread list, and their context menu exposes copyable fork details, source path, branch, isolated worktree path, lifecycle state, lifecycle execution messages, branch cleanup/archive results, inline conflict workbench state, and merge-back/discard lifecycle approvals routed through Approval Center.
- Active runtime mode is sent with natural-language requests as `metadata.runtime_mode` and as a system runtime-mode instruction.
- The legacy Python SSE gateway accepts `metadata.runtime_mode`, whitelists known mode names, and prefixes the agent task with backend mode instructions so the runtime mode affects the downstream planning/review/fix/test/commit/goal/fork behavior.
- The composer shows a context assembly preview before send, covering visible attachment chips and workspace instructions.
- `/mention selection <text>` creates a visible manual @selection context chip that is queued for the next natural-language message and then cleared with other explicit context.
- `/mcp prompt <name> <text>` creates a visible reviewed MCP prompt context chip for the next natural-language message, `/mcp sync [server]` queues Approval Center permission for bounded stdio live MCP enumeration from `.drsai/mcp-servers.json` into `.drsai/mcp-context.json`, and `/mcp resource [selector]` / `/mcp tool [selector]` imports reviewed MCP resource/tool handoff context from that file into visible chips. `/mcp exec <server> <tool> [json]` queues a separate MCP tool execution approval; approved execution performs bounded stdio `tools/call` with `shell: false`, then writes the reviewed result back into `.drsai/mcp-context.json` for explicit `/mcp tool` import.
- The desktop bridge reads a bounded `.drsai/ide-context.json` handoff inside the active workspace and exposes IDE current file/current-selection context through the same visible file and selection attachment chips.
- A source-controlled VS Code producer under `editor-integrations/vscode` listens for active editor, selection, and active-document edits, writes the current file and bounded selection into the same workspace-local handoff file, and is covered by `npm run verify:vscode-ide-producer`.
- JetBrains/Visual Studio producer skeletons under `editor-integrations/jetbrains` and `editor-integrations/visual-studio` share the same `.drsai/ide-context.json` schema, source tags, workspace-boundary checks, selection limits, temp-file replace semantics, packaged install preflight commitments, and manual desktop attach instructions; covered by `npm run verify:ide-producers`.
- The composer estimates context size for each visible source, aligns the aggregate budget with selected model `token_limit` / `max_tokens` when available, optionally calibrates the estimate from bounded model-catalog tokenizer samples, reserves output tokens, shows a fallback budget when model limits are unknown, and displays the aggregate budget state before submit.
- The permission boundary has a typed shared execution policy covering chat model calls, browser reads/interactions, workspace diff/stage/revert, terminal writes, shell commands, commits, network, and external services.
- `/permissions` exposes the current git commit policy; `/commit <message>` runs a staged-diff preflight, captures the most recent terminal test/verification result for the workspace when available, blocks empty staged commits, queues a `git.commit` approval proposal for the selected workspace with the preflight detail and structured staged-file checklist, requires local review confirmation in Approval Center, and only executes `git commit` from the main process after approval.
- `/memory add <note>` persists workspace-scoped project memory under the desktop data directory, `/memory edit <index|id> <note>` updates one entry, `/memory delete <index|id>` removes one entry, `/memory retrospective <note>` captures a post-task lesson, `/memory` lists the current workspace's reviewable memory, `/memory clear` removes all entries, and later natural-language chat includes recent project memory as an explicit system section.
- `/command add <name> = <prompt>` persists workspace-scoped reusable prompt templates under the desktop data directory, `/command` lists them, `/command delete <name>` removes them, and direct `/<custom> [args]` invocation expands the template back into the composer for review instead of auto-sending. Templates support `{{args}}`; args are appended under an Arguments section when the placeholder is absent.
- Skills Square now includes a workspace-scoped Project Memory review panel for listing, adding, editing, deleting, saving retrospectives, marking entries as skill-promotion candidates, generating reviewable `SKILL.md` skill draft files from memory entries, and installing reviewed drafts into a safe local desktop skills directory.
- Skills Square now includes a Workflow Marketplace section with starter templates for plan/review/fix, test/commit, memory-to-skill, and connector digest workflows, including triggers, steps, approval requirements, risk, verification commitments, and approval-gated workflow run recipe preparation for available templates.
- Workflow run preparation now returns a structured recipe with chat commands, manual review checkpoints, terminal verification commitments, and Approval Center queue status; `test-and-commit` routes through the shared `workflow.run` approval policy before it is considered ready.
- Prepared workflow recipes can now be started into a durable workspace-scoped run queue under `DRSAI_HOME/desktop/workflow-runs.json`, exposing per-step execution state for chat commands, terminal commands, manual checkpoints, and Approval Center follow-up without bypassing the existing approval boundaries.
- Workflow run steps now expose a dispatch hook: chat-command steps are sent into the chat composer through a renderer event and marked complete in the durable run store, manual checkpoints can be marked complete, and terminal-command steps return the command with an explicit instruction to use the existing terminal approval path rather than writing to a pty directly.
- Workflow chat command steps dispatch into the chat bar, and workflow terminal command steps dispatch into the terminal proposal drawer, where the existing command risk preview and shell approval path still control execution; Approval Center shell approval marks the terminal workflow step as running, and terminal command completion calls back into the workflow run store and advances the run on exit code 0 or blocks it on non-zero exit.
- A durable workspace-scoped background task queue persists under the desktop data directory, mirrors workflow run start/running/waiting/completed/blocked states, and appears in Skills Square as a Background tasks status panel with source, current step, message, and verification commitment.
- A durable workspace-scoped scheduled/monitor definition store persists under `DRSAI_HOME/desktop/scheduled-tasks.json`, exposes main/preload/mock API methods for list/create/update/run-due, and appears in Skills Square with cadence, target, approval, pause/enable, due-scan, active workflow run link, and verification commitment state.
- The Electron main process starts a stoppable scheduled-task worker after app readiness; it runs an initial restart scan, repeats due scans on a bounded interval, reuses the same approval-gated workflow runtime, records worker status telemetry, and mirrors started runs into the background task queue without bypassing Approval Center.
- Skills Square shows scheduler worker status telemetry including enabled/running state, next scan, and last scan counts including reconnected monitor workflows. Blocked monitor definitions expose a Resume action that re-enables the monitor and schedules it for the next due scan.
- Due scheduled monitor scans now persist `activeWorkflowRunId` / status metadata on the monitor definition. If a later scan finds that active workflow run still running, restart-recovered, or waiting in Approval Center, it reports the monitor as reconnected instead of starting a duplicate run; if the active run is blocked, the monitor is blocked with the run detail; if the run is complete or missing, the stale link is cleared before the next dispatch.
- Persisted workflow runs are recovered on app restart with an explicit `resumePlan`: chat-command, terminal-command, and manual-review steps become resumable through their existing chat bar, terminal approval, or manual checkpoint paths, while Approval Center steps remain waiting instead of being auto-executed.
- The main-process IPC layer now enforces that policy before terminal create/write and workspace stage/revert/hunk mutations; plan mode maps to read-only and blocks these side effects.
- The Approval Center navigation item shows the current shared execution-policy matrix for chat, browser, workspace, terminal, git, workflow, network, and external-service actions.
- Git commit approvals require staged-file scope, unstaged-change risk, and test-commitment confirmation before the Approve action becomes available.
- Browser-use task approvals are indexed in the main process, mirrored into a unified pending-approval queue, exposed as a pending approval snapshot, streamed into Approval Center, and routed through unified approve/reject IPC while keeping the browser-specific approval API compatible.
- Agent-proposed terminal commands now use `requestShellCommandApproval`; confirm mode queues a `shell.command` request in Approval Center and the main process writes to the target terminal only after the matching approval is accepted. Auto mode executes immediately through the same policy path.
- Files panel stage/revert file and hunk actions now enter the unified Approval Center in confirm mode and execute from the main process only after approval.
- Git, workflow, network, and connector producers have a typed `proposeApproval` bridge that evaluates the shared execution policy before either blocking, allowing, or queuing the proposal in the unified Approval Center list; git commit now also has a concrete `requestGitCommitApproval` producer fed by `/commit` staged-diff preflight.
- Channels now exposes a typed channel adapter abstraction through the main/preload bridge and renderer, with staged entries for mobile chat, Slack, GitHub, Docs, Calendar, Database, voice input, and the existing file/image input path. The file/image input adapter has a read-only channel context import runtime that scans bounded workspace-local files/images into visible summaries, including SRT/VTT timed transcript previews, selected `.eml` email message previews, selected MBOX mailbox archive previews, and selected `.vcf` / `.vcard` vCard contact previews, then lets the user explicitly attach those summaries to the current chat as visible channel-import context chips before any send. GitHub now has a workspace-scoped local Git remote connector configuration path, persists read-only connection metadata, and imports repository/branch/status/recent-commit context from local Git metadata without network access. Docs and Calendar can also import bounded workspace-local `.drsai/docs-context.json`, `.drsai/calendar-context.json`, and `.drsai/calendar-context.ics` snapshots into visible document/meeting context chips without OAuth or network calls. Database can import bounded workspace-local `.drsai/database-context.json` snapshots and `.drsai/database-schema.sql` DDL dumps with local relationship plus column/type/index/unique/check semantic hints into visible `database_table` context chips without credentials, live database connections, SQL execution, or external schema inference services. Voice input now supports microphone recording through typed IPC plus reviewed `.drsai/voice-context.json` transcript handoff import, while provider credential live acceptance, offline STT packaging, and broad device validation remain gated. OAuth-backed live provider connectors remain gated until account authorization is wired.

## Current Agent Workflow Slice

Design: enforce the shared execution policy at the privileged IPC boundary, while routing explicit side-effect proposals through the Approval Center when the policy requires confirmation. Agent-proposed shell commands, Files panel workspace stage/revert actions, `/commit <message>` git commits, approval-required workflow run recipes, fork queue-start requests, and fork merge-back/discard lifecycle actions enter Approval Center first and execute only after approval. `/commit` now runs a read-only staged-diff preflight before queuing approval, so empty staged commits are blocked and Approval Center receives the same staged files, grouped file review, diff size, unstaged-change risk, test commitment, and latest terminal test/verification result shown in chat; the Approve action is gated until staged-file scope, unstaged-change risk, and test commitment are locally confirmed. `plan_mode` maps to `read_only`, so the same paths are blocked when the user is planning or the workspace is untrusted. Slash command mode actions are held as first-class chat adapter state, displayed in the composer, sent with the next model request as explicit runtime metadata, and consumed by the legacy Python SSE gateway as whitelisted backend task guidance; `/fork <intent>` additionally asks the main process to prepare an isolated branch-backed Git worktree under the desktop data directory, creates a local `agent_run` fork thread pointing at that worktree, persists source/worktree/branch/base metadata with an active lifecycle state, reports source dirty-state risk, switches the app to it without sharing the source workspace, exposes copyable fork details from the thread menu for lifecycle review, and routes merge-back/discard requests through Approval Center before executing clean-worktree merge-back or desktop-managed worktree cleanup. `/fork queue <task1; task2>` marks each fork thread with queue group/index/status metadata, supports optional `@agent` prefixes per subtask for dispatch assignment, queues `fork.queue_start` approval before the queue can move from waiting to ready, and refreshes thread state to `ready` or `blocked` after the Approval Center decision; `/fork dispatch` is the explicit later step that starts approved ready subtasks as agent runs inside their isolated worktrees and records running/completed/blocked queue states. Merge conflicts and dirty worktrees keep the fork in `merge_pending` with a lifecycle message; successful merge records `merged` and marks branch cleanup pending, and successful cleanup records `closed` plus branch cleanup status. Cleanup removes the controlled worktree, deletes already-merged fork branches with safe `git branch -d`, and archives unmerged discarded branches under `drsai/archive/...` instead of dropping work. Project memory is explicit and workspace-scoped: `/memory add <note>` writes a bounded, validated entry to the main-process desktop data store, `/memory edit <index|id> <note>` updates one entry, `/memory delete <index|id>` removes one entry, `/memory retrospective <note>` captures task lessons, `/memory` lists recent entries before they influence a task, Skills Square provides a visual review/edit/delete surface, skill-promotion candidates can be turned into reviewable desktop-data `SKILL.md` draft files, reviewed drafts can be installed into a safe local desktop skills target, and natural-language sends include recent project memory as a visible system section. Background tasks are a durable status layer rather than a side-effect bypass: workflow runs mirror into a workspace-scoped background task queue, while terminal/chat dispatch still goes through the existing workflow and approval paths. Scheduled monitors persist cadence, target, approval, and verification metadata; their manual due-scan and the main-process real clock/restart worker both start eligible workflow runs through the same approval-gated workflow path and advance `nextRunAt` without bypassing Approval Center. On app restart, persisted in-flight workflow runs are converted into explicit restart resume plans: chat steps can be re-sent, terminal steps can be re-prepared through approval, manual checkpoints can be confirmed, and Approval Center steps remain waiting. Skills Square displays worker status telemetry, restart resume state, and a Resume action for blocked monitor definitions; the remaining scheduler gap is fully autonomous external process reconnect after restart. The Approval Center derives policy rows from the same shared evaluator and consumes a unified pending-approval queue exposed by the main process, seeded by browser-use action proposals, agent shell command proposals, Files panel workspace mutation proposals, git commit proposals, workflow run proposals, fork queue-start proposals, fork lifecycle proposals, and by a generic proposal bridge for future network/connector producers.

Implementation: `src/main/executionPolicyGate.ts` now builds policy from My DrSai config and exposes `assertExecutionAllowed`; `src/main/index.ts` calls it before terminal create/write, indexes live browser-use approvals, mirrors them into a typed `DesktopPendingApproval` queue, exposes `desktop:propose-approval` for non-browser runtime proposals, exposes `desktop:shell-command-approval` for agent-proposed terminal commands, marks workflow terminal steps running after the approved shell command is written, exposes `desktop:git-commit-approval` for policy-gated commits, includes commit preflight body and structured checklist in approval detail, routes Files panel stage/revert file/hunk actions through pending workspace mutation approvals, exposes `desktop:fork-queue-start-approval` for queue-start gating and thread queue-state updates, exposes `desktop:fork-queue-dispatch` for explicit dispatch of approved ready fork queue threads into isolated-worktree agent runs with per-thread agent assignment metadata, exposes project-memory list/add/update/clear plus project-skill-draft list/create/install IPC, exposes workflow marketplace list plus `desktop:workflow-run-prepare` recipe IPC, exposes `desktop:workflow-run-start`/`desktop:workflow-runs-list`/`desktop:workflow-run-step-complete` for the durable workflow run queue, recovers persisted workflow runs after restart and mirrors recovered resume state to background tasks, exposes `desktop:background-tasks-list`/`desktop:background-task-enqueue`/`desktop:background-task-update` for the background task queue, exposes `desktop:scheduled-tasks-list`/`desktop:scheduled-task-create`/`desktop:scheduled-task-update` for scheduled monitor definitions, starts the scheduled-task worker after app readiness, mirrors workflow run status changes into that queue, and exposes `desktop:ide-context` for bounded IDE current file/current-selection handoff snapshots. `src/main/agentRuns.ts` updates fork queue metadata to completed or blocked when dispatched agent runs finish. `src/main/backgroundTasks.ts` persists bounded background tasks by stable workspace key under `DRSAI_HOME/desktop/background-tasks.json`, validates list/enqueue/update requests, and maps workflow run lifecycle states into background task states. `src/main/scheduledTasks.ts` persists bounded scheduled/monitor definitions by stable workspace key under `DRSAI_HOME/desktop/scheduled-tasks.json`, validates list/create/update requests, runs due workflow scans, reconnects monitor definitions to active or restart-recovered workflow runs, and exposes a stoppable non-overlapping worker for clock/restart scans without adding a side-effect bypass. `src/main/ideContext.ts` reads `.drsai/ide-context.json` from inside the active workspace, validates workspace-relative paths, bounds selection text, and returns a typed `DesktopIdeContextSnapshot`. `src/main/channelAdapters.ts` defines the first staged channel adapter catalog for mobile chat, Slack, GitHub, Docs, Calendar, voice input, and file/image input, and `desktop:channel-adapters-list` exposes it through the preload bridge. `src/main/workflowMarketplace.ts` defines starter workflow templates for plan/review/fix, test/commit, memory-to-skill, and connector digest flows with trigger, approval, risk, steps, required capabilities, and verification metadata, then builds structured run recipes for available templates. `src/main/workflowRuns.ts` converts prepared recipes into per-step execution state for chat commands, terminal commands, manual reviews, and approval waits, persists bounded runs by stable workspace key under `DRSAI_HOME/desktop/workflow-runs.json`, restores them through `listWorkflowRuns`, marks terminal steps running after shell approval, records terminal step completion by exit code, and builds restart resume plans without automatically performing side effects. `src/main/projectMemory.ts` persists bounded project memory entries under `DRSAI_HOME/desktop/project-memory.json` by stable workspace key and validates single-entry edits/deletes by id. `src/main/projectSkills.ts` renders bounded project-memory content into `SKILL.md` drafts under `DRSAI_HOME/desktop/skill-drafts`, stores a workspace-scoped index under `DRSAI_HOME/desktop/project-skill-drafts.json`, and installs reviewed drafts under `DRSAI_HOME/desktop/installed-skills/<workspace-key>/<slug>/SKILL.md` without accepting arbitrary target paths. `TerminalPanel` submits proposed command runs through that bridge instead of directly writing to the pty, preserves workflow metadata in shell approvals, records completed terminal test/verification commands as the workspace's latest test result, and reports workflow terminal command completion back to the run store. `PatchReviewPanel` treats queued workspace mutations as waiting in Approval Center. `src/renderer/src/adapters/useDesktopChatAdapter.ts` now stores runtime mode from slash commands, injects it into `metadata.runtime_mode`, runs `/commit <message>` staged-diff preflight, includes the latest terminal test result in chat and Approval Center checklist detail, queues commit approvals only when staged changes exist, handles `/memory add`, `/memory edit`, `/memory delete`, `/memory retrospective`, and `/memory clear`, creates `/fork queue` subtask threads with queue metadata and optional `@agent` assignment metadata, queues fork queue-start approval, dispatches approved ready queue threads through `/fork dispatch`, loads current workspace memory, and adds runtime-mode plus project-memory system sections; `ChatWorkspace` shows the active mode and clear control plus explicit IDE current file/current selection attachment controls. `cores/python/packages/drsai/src/drsai/backend/gateway.py` now declares desktop request metadata, extracts `runtime_mode`, rejects unknown mode names, and prefixes the agent task with mode-specific backend guidance. `src/renderer/src/components/SkillSquareView.tsx` now receives the active workspace path and offers a Project Memory review panel backed by the same desktop API for add/edit/delete/retrospective, promotion-candidate capture, skill draft generation/listing, local installation of reviewed drafts, a Workflow Marketplace section backed by the marketplace catalog, recipe preparation actions, workflow start action, persisted run restoration, restart resume-plan rendering, terminal running/completion update events, run status rendering, a Background tasks panel backed by the durable queue, and a Scheduled monitors panel backed by the scheduler store. `src/renderer/src/components/ApprovalCenterView.tsx` renders the policy-derived action matrix including `workflow.run` and `fork.queue_start`, polls pending unified approvals, shows browser events immediately, expands git commit approvals into a grouped staged-file review checklist, disables approval until required checklist items are confirmed, broadcasts refreshed workflow run state after approved shell commands, refreshes thread state after fork lifecycle and queue-start approvals, and is exposed through the Workspace navigation. `src/renderer/src/components/ChannelsView.tsx` shows grouped chat, connector, and input adapters with configured/approval status.

Current run addendum: `desktop:workspace-checkpoint-create` now captures a bounded manifest of current Git-changed workspace files into `DRSAI_HOME/desktop/workspace-checkpoints`, `desktop:workspace-checkpoints-list` restores recent checkpoint visibility, and `desktop:workspace-checkpoint-restore` routes restore through the shared `workspace.revert` Approval Center policy before copying stored files back or removing paths that were absent at checkpoint time. Files context now includes a Rollback checkpoint panel for create/list/restore handoff, and mock/preload/shared API surfaces match the main-process contract. `desktop:channel-context-import` also exposes a bounded read-only file/image channel import path for `file-input`; it rejects unsupported adapters, validates the workspace path, skips symlinks, bounds scan depth/item count, returns visible summaries through the preload/mock bridge, renders the imported context in `ChannelsView`, and adds an explicit `Attach to chat` handoff that converts reviewed summaries into visible `channel-import` selection chips for the current chat thread. `desktop:channel-adapter-configure` now persists workspace-scoped `local_git_remote` GitHub connector metadata under `DRSAI_HOME/desktop/channel-connections.json`, and `desktop:channel-context-import` can import read-only GitHub connector context for repository, remote, branch, HEAD, status, recent commits, and bounded issue/PR snapshot items from `.drsai/github-context.json` or an explicit workspace-local JSON path without performing OAuth or network calls. `desktop:channel-outbound-draft` now turns a connector/mobile outbound draft into a `connector` / `external.service` Approval Center proposal, validates adapter direction and workspace scope, exposes the path through preload/mock, and adds a Channels action/result surface for an approval-gated outbound channel draft without performing live provider sends.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07: `ChatWorkspace` now detects multi-item `/fork queue` input and shows a compact visual per-subtask agent assignment panel backed by the loaded agent catalog. The panel submits structured `forkQueueAgentAssignments` with the chat submit options; `useDesktopChatAdapter` applies those choices before falling back to text `@agent` prefixes, persists the chosen agent on fork metadata, and forwards it through the existing `/fork dispatch` path. `npm run verify:chat-commands` and `npm run verify:fork-worktree` now assert this visual assignment contract.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 handoff: `/fork handoff <thread-id> <agent>` now reassigns an existing queued fork subtask by updating its `queueAgentHint`, `queueAgentId`, `queueAgentName`, `queueMessage`, and `queueUpdatedAt` fork metadata through the existing thread update bridge. It deliberately does not start an agent run or bypass queue-start approval; approved ready queues still dispatch through `/fork dispatch`. `npm run verify:chat-commands` and `npm run verify:fork-worktree` assert the command copy, parser, metadata update path, and no-execution safety message.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 scheduler: `/fork schedule [limit N]` now scans the current workspace for approved ready queued fork subtasks, orders them by queue group, queue index, queue update time, and thread id, preserves stored per-thread agent assignments, and reuses `desktopApi.dispatchForkQueue` so Approval Center queue-start gating and agent-run safeguards remain unchanged. `npm run verify:chat-commands` and `npm run verify:fork-worktree` assert the command copy, scheduler handler, deterministic ordering, bounded limit parser, and dispatch reuse.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 recovery: fork threads whose lifecycle is `merge_pending` now show a thread-menu recovery block that classifies conflict, dirty source, dirty fork, or generic pending states from the lifecycle message. The menu offers no-side-effect open-source/open-fork actions, structured recovery status detail parsed from lifecycle metadata, copyable recovery/status checklists, and copyable source/fork diff inspection commands that explicitly keep merge-back behind Approval Center. `npm run verify:thread-menu` and `npm run verify:fork-worktree` assert the recovery UI, checklist copy, diff command copy, styling, and roadmap evidence.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 conflict workbench: conflict-classified fork recovery now parses conflicted source files from lifecycle status lines into an inline conflict workbench in the thread menu. Each parsed file shows status, path, and the required resolution state, can load a content merge editor with source workspace preview, fork worktree preview, source diff preview, and an editable manual resolved draft for copy-out, and can queue resolved-file staging through the existing Approval Center workspace mutation path. The menu also adds a copyable conflict resolution plan with per-file diff, optional ours/theirs commands, staging reminders, test reminder, and explicit Approval Center merge-back boundary. `npm run verify:thread-menu` and `npm run verify:fork-worktree` assert the parser, content preview/editor UI, Approval Center staging copy, resolution-plan copy action, styling, and roadmap evidence.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 draft write-back: the fork conflict workbench now keeps source/fork whole-file draft controls, can request Approval Center-gated resolved-draft write-back through `desktop:fork-conflict-draft-write`, validates the active thread fork state, source workspace allow-list, path boundary, draft size, and reviewed diff hash before queueing, then rechecks the diff hash again before the approved file overwrite. Writing the draft does not stage it; users still review the refreshed diff and use the existing Stage resolved file approval path. `npm run verify:thread-menu` and `npm run verify:fork-worktree` assert the shared API, preload/mock bridge, main-process pending approval path, UI controls, styling, and roadmap evidence.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 hunk controls: the fork conflict workbench now parses standard Git conflict markers inside the manual resolved draft, shows each conflict hunk with source-side and fork-side previews, supports per-hunk source/fork/both application, and supports bulk apply-all source/fork hunk actions. These controls only mutate the local draft; file writes still require the existing Approval Center-gated draft write-back and resolved-file staging path. `npm run verify:thread-menu` and `npm run verify:fork-worktree` assert the parser, per-hunk controls, bulk controls, styling hooks, and roadmap evidence.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 semantic preview: the fork conflict workbench now computes a local semantic three-way merge review preview from the loaded source workspace content, fork worktree content, resolved draft, and fork base ref. It highlights source/fork/draft line and conflict-marker signals, risk level, review focus, file-type-aware test suggestions, and per-hunk test suggestions before write-back. This remains a read-only renderer aid; draft write-back and staging still go through Approval Center. `npm run verify:thread-menu` and `npm run verify:fork-worktree` assert the semantic preview helpers, hunk-level test suggestion helpers, UI copy, styles, and roadmap evidence.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 merge-base preview: the fork conflict workbench now loads a bounded read-only `git show <baseRef>:<path>` preview through `desktop:workspace-git-file-at-ref`, validates the workspace path and ref input in the main process, and shows Base/Source/Fork columns before draft editing. The semantic merge preview now compares source and fork line deltas against the true merge-base content instead of using only the base ref label. `npm run verify:thread-menu` and `npm run verify:fork-worktree` assert the shared API, main/preload/mock bridge, renderer loader, UI copy, and roadmap evidence.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 repo test graph: the fork conflict semantic preview now maps conflicted file paths to likely repository verification commands, including `npm run verify:*` scripts for matching verification modules plus renderer/node typecheck coverage for affected source folders. The same repo test graph matches feed hunk-level suggestions so reviewers see concrete commands before draft write-back or staging. This remains a read-only renderer heuristic; parser-backed AST dependency graph discovery is still future work. `npm run verify:thread-menu`, `npm run verify:fork-worktree`, and `npm run typecheck:web` assert the helper, UI copy, styles, roadmap evidence, and TypeScript validity.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 AST-aware structure diff: the fork conflict semantic preview now adds a read-only AST-aware structure diff section. It scans common structural declarations for TypeScript/JavaScript, Python, CSS, Markdown, and configuration files, compares source/fork/resolved draft symbols against the true merge-base preview, flags overlapping structural edits, and raises review risk when both sides touch the same structure. This remains a lightweight renderer scanner rather than a parser-backed AST engine; write-back and staging still stay behind Approval Center. `npm run verify:thread-menu`, `npm run verify:fork-worktree`, and `npm run typecheck:web` assert the helpers, UI copy, styles, roadmap evidence, and TypeScript validity.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 parser-backed fork conflict analysis: fork conflict structure scanning, conflict hunk parsing, file-kind detection, hunk-level test suggestions, and repo test graph matching now live in `src/renderer/src/components/forkConflictAnalysis.ts` instead of being embedded in `WorkspaceShell`. The analyzer adds brace/indent/Markdown/config parser labels and scoped symbol evidence to the semantic preview, while repo test recommendations now come from a maintained `FORK_CONFLICT_TEST_GRAPH` manifest rather than scattered renderer `if` checks. This keeps conflict review read-only and still routes draft write-back/staging through Approval Center; the remaining gap is replacing the local parsers with compiler-backed ASTs where practical and generating the graph from package/import metadata. `npm run verify:thread-menu`, `npm run verify:fork-worktree`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover the module contract, UI wiring, roadmap evidence, and TypeScript validity.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 folder context: `desktop:workspace-folder-summary` now builds a bounded read-only folder summary for explicit folder attachments, skipping noisy directories, limiting depth/entries/sample files, sampling small text outlines, estimating prompt tokens, and exposing the result through shared/preload/mock API. `ChatWorkspace` converts selected folder chips into reviewed `visibleText` summaries before send, and `src/main/chat.ts` only inlines folder context when that reviewed summary is present; otherwise it preserves a `folder-summary-missing` non-inline reason. `npm run verify:context-assembler`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover the API, bridge, mock, composer, chat injection, and TypeScript validity.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 inline mentions: `ChatWorkspace` now parses explicit inline `@file:<path>` and `@folder:<path>` composer mentions, resolves relative paths against the active workspace passed from `App`, deduplicates them with visible attachment chips, includes them in the context preview and budget, and prepares `@folder` mentions through the same bounded reviewed summary path before submit. The design deliberately requires the `@file:` / `@folder:` prefix so normal prose with `@name` is not silently treated as filesystem context. `npm run verify:context-assembler` asserts the parser, workspace path handoff, submit-before-summary ordering, and roadmap evidence; `npm run typecheck:web` covers renderer TypeScript.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 IDE packaging preflight: JetBrains and Visual Studio IDE producer skeletons now share a checked-in `editor-integrations/packaging-manifest.json` that records package descriptors, source files, source tags, handoff path, selection limit, package preflight steps, and manual validation commitments. Their READMEs now include packaged install smoke guidance, and `npm run verify:ide-producers` asserts the manifest, README packaging sections, source files, descriptor files, source tags, workspace-bound handoff behavior, and roadmap evidence. Live IDE packaging/debug runs remain a manual environment-dependent validation step.

Tests: `npm run verify:execution-policy` verifies the shared contract including `fork.queue_start`, `workspace.checkpoint`, terminal/workspace policy gates, and agent shell command approval path; `npm run verify:workspace-checkpoints` verifies the workspace checkpoint shared API, main-process bounded store, path-bound manifest restore, Approval Center-gated restore, preload/mock bridge, Files panel UI, Approval Center policy row, styling hooks, package script, and roadmap evidence; `npm run verify:approval-center` verifies the approval center route, policy evaluator usage, unified pending-approval API, shell command approval API, Files panel workspace mutation queueing, generic proposal API, browser-use approval mirroring, git commit preflight detail, fork queue-start approval wiring, workflow run approval policy wiring, recent terminal test result wiring, required guided checklist gate, polling, and styling hooks; `npm run verify:project-memory` verifies the project-memory shared API, main/preload IPC, bounded persistent store, `/memory add`, `/memory edit`, `/memory delete`, `/memory retrospective`, and `/memory clear` command handling, chat system-context injection, Skills Square visual review/edit/delete/promote-candidate and skill-draft wiring, skill draft `SKILL.md` rendering, reviewed local install, mock bridge, and roadmap update; `npm run verify:workflow-marketplace` verifies the workflow template shared API, main/preload IPC, starter templates, approval and verification metadata, structured run recipe creation, approval-gated workflow preparation, durable workspace-scoped workflow run persistence, restart resume metadata and recovery wiring, Skills Square run start/restoration/rendering, chat and terminal dispatch events, shell approval-to-running status feedback, terminal completion callbacks, mock bridge, styles, and roadmap evidence; `npm run verify:background-tasks` verifies the background task shared API, bounded workspace-scoped persistent store, main/preload IPC, workflow-run lifecycle mirroring, mock bridge, Skills Square status panel, styling hooks, and roadmap evidence; `npm run verify:scheduled-tasks` verifies the scheduler shared API, bounded workspace-scoped `scheduled-tasks.json` persistent store, main/preload IPC, mock bridge, Skills Square scheduler panel, create/pause controls, approval-gated manual due-scan, active and restart-recovered workflow run reconnect semantics, main-process worker lifecycle, non-overlapping bounded worker scans, background-task mirroring, styling hooks, and roadmap evidence; `npm run verify:channel-adapters` verifies the channel adapter shared API, workspace-scoped local Git remote GitHub connector configuration, read-only GitHub connector context import, main/preload IPC, enabled Channels navigation, renderer status surface, mock bridge, imported-context attach-to-chat handoff, approval-gated outbound channel draft proposal path, styling hooks, and roadmap evidence; `npm run verify:context-assembler` verifies visible context assembly, folder summary API/bridge/mock, bounded main-process folder scanning, reviewed folder summary attachment, chat-side folder summary inlining, @selection context, and budget preview;
