`npm run verify:fork-worktree` verifies the shared/main/preload/mock fork worktree API, branch-backed Git worktree creation, dirty-source warning, fail-closed renderer behavior, persisted thread fork metadata, safe branch deletion/archive policy, fork queue-start approval, explicit agent dispatch for ready fork queues, per-thread `@agent` assignment forwarding, running/completed/blocked queue-state persistence, fork thread list marker, thread menu fork detail actions, and roadmap evidence; `npm run typecheck:node` verifies main-process TypeScript; `npm run verify:chat-commands` and `npm run typecheck:web` verify runtime mode command state, `/skills` navigation and focused skill/workflow template selection in Skills Square, `/fork` isolated worktree-backed agent-run thread creation/switching, `/fork queue @agent` parsing and dispatch assignment wiring, `/commit` preflight wiring, terminal test-result capture, memory command wiring, custom command expansion, and renderer TypeScript.

IDE producer packaging coverage: `npm run verify:ide-producers` also verifies `editor-integrations/packaging-manifest.json`, packaged install preflight commitments, and JetBrains/Visual Studio README packaging guidance.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 checkpoint preview: rollback checkpoints now expose a read-only checkpoint diff preview before restore. The main process validates workspace/checkpoint scope, compares stored snapshot hashes with current workspace files, reports added/modified/deleted/unchanged/skipped entries with bounded text snippets, and the Files panel shows a `Preview diff` action separate from Approval Center-gated restore. `npm run verify:workspace-checkpoints`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover the shared API, main/preload/mock bridge, Files panel UI, styling, and roadmap evidence.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 Docs/Calendar snapshots: `desktop:channel-context-import` now supports `docs-connector` and `calendar-connector` workspace-local snapshot handoffs. The main process reads bounded `.drsai/docs-context.json` and `.drsai/calendar-context.json` files or an explicit workspace-local `snapshotPath`, rejects symlinks/out-of-workspace paths, normalizes document and meeting records into visible channel-import context items, and keeps live provider sync/send behind future OAuth/session work. `ChannelsView` exposes `Import context` for Docs and Calendar, the mock bridge includes matching preview fixtures, and `npm run verify:channel-adapters` asserts the API, runtime, UI, mock, and roadmap evidence.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 outbound delivery ledger: Approval Center connector sends now have an explicit post-approval runtime boundary. `desktop:channel-outbound-draft` stores approved drafts as pending connector runtime work, `desktop:decide-approval` records approved drafts as `blocked` when live provider credentials/runtime are not configured, rejected drafts as `rejected`, and `desktop:channel-outbound-deliveries` exposes the durable workspace-scoped delivery ledger to Channels. The renderer and mock bridge show recent outbound runtime outcomes so approval after-effects are visible and testable without performing network sends. `npm run verify:channel-adapters` asserts the shared API, main/preload IPC, mock behavior, UI ledger, styles, and roadmap evidence.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 provider sessions: Channel adapters now include a workspace-scoped provider session stub configuration mode for Slack, GitHub, Docs, and Calendar. The main process persists account label, scope label, credential-state, optional expiry, and read-only metadata in the same bounded connection store without storing provider tokens, starting OAuth, performing network sync, or sending messages. Channels exposes a `Configure session` action alongside GitHub's local Git remote configuration, and the mock bridge mirrors the same no-secret contract. `npm run verify:channel-adapters`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover the shared API, main runtime, preload/mock/renderer contract, UI evidence, roadmap evidence, and TypeScript validity.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 inbound events: Channel imports now register a durable workspace-scoped inbound channel event queue under the desktop data directory. GitHub, Docs, Calendar, and file/image imports become reviewed local events that Channels can list and explicitly route into the existing visible chat context handoff without calling external providers. The main/preload/mock bridge exposes list and route APIs, and the route operation marks the event as routed while returning the same `DesktopChannelContextImportResult` shape used by `Attach to chat`. `npm run verify:channel-adapters`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover the shared API, main persistent store, preload/mock bridge, Channels UI, styles, roadmap evidence, and TypeScript validity.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 connector snapshot sync: Channels now exposes explicit workspace-local connector snapshot sync for GitHub, Docs, and Calendar handoff files. `desktop:channel-snapshot-sync` polls bounded `.drsai/*-context.json` snapshots, reuses the existing reviewed import normalizers, records stable de-duplicated inbound channel events, preserves routed/dismissed state for unchanged snapshot events, and reports that no OAuth flow, secret access, provider network call, or outbound send was performed. The preload/mock bridge and Channels UI expose the action and result panel. This partially advances inbound provider event polling while live provider OAuth/session polling remains future work. `npm run verify:channel-adapters`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover the shared API, main runtime, IPC, preload/mock bridge, UI, styles, roadmap evidence, and TypeScript validity.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 selected file import: Channels `file-input` now has a `Pick files` path that sends explicit selected file paths through `DesktopChannelContextImportRequest.paths`. The main process only accepts workspace-local, non-symlink, allow-listed files, summarizes readable text and notebook content, reports skipped/out-of-scope selections as truncation, records the reviewed import in the inbound event queue, and still requires explicit Attach/Route to chat before the content enters the composer. `npm run verify:channel-adapters`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover the shared API, main runtime, renderer action, mock bridge, roadmap evidence, and TypeScript validity.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 voice transcript input: Channels `voice-input` now has a read-only local transcript handoff path. `desktop:channel-context-import` reads bounded workspace-local `.drsai/voice-context.json`, `voiceTranscriptPath`, or plain text transcript files, rejects symlinks/out-of-workspace paths, normalizes transcript records into `voice_transcript` channel context items, records reviewed imports in the inbound event queue, and still requires explicit Attach/Route to chat before transcript content enters the composer. This does not start microphone capture, transcription services, provider OAuth, network calls, or outbound sends. `npm run verify:channel-adapters`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover the shared API, main runtime, renderer action, mock bridge, roadmap evidence, and TypeScript validity.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 mobile chat handoff: Channels `mobile-chat` now has a read-only local mobile message handoff path. `desktop:channel-context-import` reads bounded workspace-local `.drsai/mobile-context.json` or an explicit workspace-local `mobileSnapshotPath`/`snapshotPath`, rejects symlinks/out-of-workspace paths, normalizes phone-originated message records into `mobile_message` channel context items, records reviewed imports in the inbound event queue, and includes mobile handoffs in the existing snapshot sync action. This does not start device pairing, push notifications, mobile endpoint hosting, provider OAuth, network calls, or outbound sends. `npm run verify:channel-adapters`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover the shared API, main runtime, renderer action, mock bridge, roadmap evidence, and TypeScript validity.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 inbound dismiss: Channels now exposes a local `Dismiss` action beside `Route to chat` for queued inbound channel events. Dismiss uses the existing `desktop:channel-inbound-route` API with `action: "dismiss"`, updates the durable inbound event ledger, keeps routed/dismissed events locked from repeated processing, and performs no provider action or network call. `npm run verify:channel-adapters` asserts the UI action, mock behavior, local-ledger safety copy, styles, and roadmap evidence.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 MCP prompt context: `/mcp prompt <name> <text>` now queues reviewed MCP prompt context as a visible selection-style context chip for the next natural-language message. The command keeps the same explicit context boundary as `/mention selection`, truncates oversized prompt text, and does not connect to or execute an MCP server. `npm run verify:chat-commands` asserts the command handler, usage copy, source note, visible attachment action, and no-execution boundary.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 MCP handoff context: `/mcp resource [selector]` and `/mcp tool [selector]` now import reviewed MCP resource/tool context from a bounded workspace-local `.drsai/mcp-context.json` handoff. The main process rejects missing, oversized, symlinked, or out-of-workspace handoffs, normalizes direct or per-server `resources`/`tools` entries, and the renderer attaches imported items as visible selection chips for the next natural-language message. This still does not connect to, enumerate, or execute an MCP server; it establishes the explicit reviewable attachment path that a future permissioned live MCP runtime can feed. `npm run verify:chat-commands`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover the shared API, main/preload/mock bridge, command copy, adapter attachment behavior, and TypeScript validity.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 permissioned live MCP bridge: `/mcp sync [server]` now queues Approval Center permission for live MCP `resources/list` and `tools/list` enumeration using workspace-local `.drsai/mcp-servers.json`. Approved enumeration runs through a bounded stdio bridge with `shell: false`, writes reviewed results into `.drsai/mcp-context.json`, and then reuses `/mcp resource` / `/mcp tool` for visible context-chip import. `/mcp exec <server> <tool> [json]` queues a separate MCP tool execution approval; after approval the main process performs bounded stdio MCP `tools/call`, validates JSON object input, records the result as reviewed MCP tool context in `.drsai/mcp-context.json`, and keeps context import read-only. `npm run verify:mcp-live-bridge`, `npm run verify:chat-commands`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover the shared API, main approval chain, approved execution path, preload/mock bridge, slash command wiring, roadmap evidence, and TypeScript validity.

## Next Small Steps

1. Manually load `editor-integrations/vscode`, run the JetBrains packaged install smoke, and debug the Visual Studio VSIX smoke to verify `.drsai/ide-context.json` attaches as IDE current file/current selection in the desktop composer.
2. Extend reviewed connector authorization preparation into real OAuth/device polling and token exchange, then extend the GitHub connector from local Git metadata into OAuth-backed read-only issue/PR sync and reuse the same approval path for outbound provider sends.
3. Replace the local parser-backed fork conflict scanner with compiler-backed AST diffing where available, and extend the generated repository import index with package/export metadata and additional non-TypeScript import sources.
4. Extend the approved MCP tool execution runtime with real third-party reusable MCP server smoke coverage and provider-owned reconnect diagnostics beyond local reusable-pool restart evidence.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 external runtime reconnect: Workflow recipes now support an explicit `external_runtime` step kind and `reconnect_external` restart resume action. The marketplace includes an `external-runtime-reconnect` recipe, the main workflow run engine persists and recovers those steps without auto-starting provider processes, Skills Square shows a `Reconnect runtime` action, and the mock bridge mirrors the contract. This advances autonomous external runtime reconnect from not implemented to a safe partial recovery contract; real provider process supervision and automatic reconnect remain future work. `npm run verify:workflow-marketplace`, `npm run verify:background-tasks`, `npm run verify:scheduled-tasks`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 MCP execution audit: approved `/mcp exec <server> <tool> [json]` now writes a workspace-local `.drsai/mcp-execution-audit.json` ledger for completed and failed runtime executions, rejected MCP tool approvals are recorded without starting the server, and Approval Center shows the current workspace's recent MCP execution history with explicit `/mcp tool <result>` import shortcuts for reviewed results. The ledger rejects symlink/non-file write targets and remains separate from `.drsai/mcp-context.json` so result attachment still requires an explicit context import. `npm run verify:mcp-live-bridge`, `npm run verify:approval-center`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover the shared API, main/preload/mock bridge, Approval Center UI, route wiring, roadmap evidence, and TypeScript validity.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 MCP session lifecycle audit: live MCP enumeration and approved tool execution now record bounded session lifecycle events in workspace-local `.drsai/mcp-session-audit.json`. Enumeration records started plus completed/failed/timed_out summaries, tool execution records started plus completed/failed/timed_out summaries, and rejected tool approvals are recorded without starting stdio. Approval Center reads the new ledger beside the result audit panel so users can distinguish runtime lifecycle from reviewed context import. This is still a per-request stdio lifecycle audit, not a long-lived reusable MCP process manager. `npm run verify:mcp-live-bridge`, `npm run verify:approval-center`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover the shared API, main/preload/mock bridge, UI panel, roadmap evidence, and TypeScript validity.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 MCP queued cancellation: queued MCP live enumeration and tool execution approvals now have an explicit cancellation control before any stdio runtime starts. `/mcp cancel <approval-id>` calls the same Approval Center decision boundary with `reason: "cancel"`, Approval Center shows a `Cancel MCP` action on MCP pending approvals, the main process records cancelled enumeration/tool lifecycle events in `.drsai/mcp-session-audit.json`, and cancelled tool executions are also written to `.drsai/mcp-execution-audit.json` without writing reviewed context. Queued cancellation is separate from the running MCP session cancellation control below. `npm run verify:mcp-live-bridge`, `npm run verify:approval-center`, `npm run verify:chat-commands`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover the shared API, command path, main/preload/mock bridge, UI panel, roadmap evidence, and TypeScript validity.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 running MCP session cancellation: bounded live MCP enumeration and approved tool execution now register their stdio child process as an active workspace-scoped MCP session in the main process. Approval Center lists running MCP sessions, exposes a `Cancel MCP` control for the current workspace, and calls `desktop:mcp-session-cancel` to kill only the tracked child process. Cancelled running enumeration and tool sessions are finalized as `cancelled` lifecycle audit entries, and cancelled tool executions also write a cancelled execution audit without writing reviewed context. This is still per-request MCP process control; reusable long-lived MCP process pooling remains future work. `npm run verify:mcp-live-bridge`, `npm run verify:approval-center`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover the shared API, main runtime registry, preload/mock bridge, Approval Center UI, styles, roadmap evidence, and TypeScript validity.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 Slack snapshots: Channels `slack-chat` now has a workspace-local Slack snapshot import path. `desktop:channel-context-import` reads bounded `.drsai/slack-context.json`, `slackSnapshotPath`, or generic `snapshotPath` handoffs, rejects symlinks/out-of-workspace paths, normalizes message/thread records into `slack_message` channel context items, records reviewed imports in the inbound channel event queue, and includes Slack in explicit connector snapshot sync. This is a workspace-local Slack snapshot import only; live OAuth reads, provider polling, and actual Slack sends remain future work behind the existing approval and delivery-ledger boundaries. `npm run verify:channel-adapters`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover the shared API, main runtime, renderer action, mock bridge, roadmap evidence, and TypeScript validity.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 MCP verification resilience: the MCP live bridge verification now tracks timed-out session lifecycle support by checking the actual `timed_out` status capability instead of a brittle object-literal source layout. The approved tool execution failure path also computes a typed session status before recording the lifecycle audit, preserving failed/timed-out/cancelled behavior while making the monitoring script stable. `npm run verify:mcp-live-bridge`, `npm run verify:approval-center`, and `npm run typecheck:node` passed for this slice.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 reusable MCP sessions: live MCP enumeration and approved tool execution now support explicit reusable stdio sessions through `/mcp sync --reuse [server]` and `/mcp exec --reuse <server> <tool> [json]`. The main process keeps reuse off by default, pools only workspace/server-matched stdio children, records `reusedSession` and `sessionReuseKey` in results/session audits/tool audits, exposes running reusable requests through the existing active-session cancellation boundary, and shuts idle reusable sessions down after a bounded timeout. This partially closes long-lived MCP session reuse; remaining work is live fixture coverage against real MCP servers and richer pooled-session health UI. `npm run verify:mcp-live-bridge`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover the shared API, main reusable pool, command path, mock bridge, audit evidence, roadmap evidence, and TypeScript validity.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 reusable MCP runtime fixture: `npm run verify:mcp-live-bridge` now runs a real runtime fake stdio MCP fixture in addition to source-contract checks. The verifier creates a temporary workspace, writes `.drsai/mcp-servers.json`, starts a JSON-RPC MCP server through the live bridge, calls reusable enumeration and approved reusable `tools/call`, checks that both operations share one session reuse key and one stdio process, verifies active-session visibility while the tool call is running, and asserts reviewed `.drsai/mcp-context.json`, `.drsai/mcp-execution-audit.json`, and `.drsai/mcp-session-audit.json` evidence. Real third-party MCP server smoke coverage remains a follow-up.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 MCP result attach action: Approval Center MCP tool execution audit rows now expose an explicit `Attach result` action for completed reviewed tool results. The button reuses the existing read-only `.drsai/mcp-context.json` import path with `kind: "tool"` and the audited `resultContextName`, then adds the reviewed result as a visible chat context attachment in the current thread without reconnecting to an MCP server or executing a tool. `npm run verify:approval-center`, `npm run verify:mcp-live-bridge`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover the renderer contract, route wiring, styles, roadmap evidence, and TypeScript validity.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 MCP reusable pool health UI: Approval Center now has a read-only reusable MCP session pool panel beside active sessions and lifecycle history. The main process exposes `desktop:mcp-reusable-sessions` from the existing workspace-scoped reusable stdio pool, showing reuse key, server, command, ready/busy/idle status, pending request count, last-used time, bounded stderr preview, and idle shutdown countdown without starting, cancelling, or executing MCP work. The runtime fake stdio MCP fixture now verifies idle pool health after reusable enumeration and busy pool health while an approved reusable tool call is in flight. `npm run verify:mcp-live-bridge`, `npm run verify:approval-center`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover the shared API, main/preload/mock bridge, Approval Center UI, fixture behavior, styles, and roadmap evidence.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 MCP reusable pool close: Approval Center reusable MCP session rows now expose an explicit same-workspace close action for idle or busy pooled stdio sessions. The main process validates workspace scope and reuse key before closing only the selected reusable child process, rejecting pending pooled requests with a closed-session error and performing no enumeration or tool execution. The mock bridge mirrors the behavior, and the runtime fake stdio MCP fixture now verifies session creation, reuse, active/busy visibility, close result, and an empty reusable pool after close. `npm run verify:mcp-live-bridge`, `npm run verify:approval-center`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover the shared API, main/preload/mock bridge, Approval Center UI, fixture behavior, styles, and roadmap evidence.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 MCP reusable pool lifecycle diagnostics: reusable MCP stdio pool sessions now write workspace-scoped `reusable_pool` lifecycle entries to `.drsai/mcp-session-audit.json` when the pool process starts and when it closes because of Approval Center close, idle timeout, process exit, output limits, request timeout, or initialization failure. Approval Center history can now distinguish a workspace that has no live reusable sessions because the app restarted or the pool was closed from a workspace that never used reusable MCP. `npm run verify:mcp-live-bridge`, `npm run verify:approval-center`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover the shared API phase/status extension, main-process audit writes, runtime fixture started/closed evidence, and closed lifecycle styling.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 chatbar capability checklist: `docs/chatbar-capability-checklist.md` now tracks the requested smart chat bar 8 capabilities and MVP/P1/P2 feature points with `[x]`, `[~]`, and `[ ]` statuses, evidence, gaps, prioritized remaining work, agent workflow notes, automated verification commitments, and manual verification commitments. `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist` asserts that the checklist keeps the required capabilities, slash/context/execution/mode/multi-agent/memory/workflow/channel feature points, status markers, and test-commitment language in place for future automation runs.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 local marketplace sync: Skills Square now supports a reviewed local marketplace submission path for project skill drafts and a reviewed workspace-local workflow template sync path. `desktop:project-skill-draft-publish` writes `submission.json` plus `SKILL.md` under the desktop data directory without uploading or installing anything, while `desktop:workflow-marketplace-sync` imports bounded templates only from workspace-local `.drsai/workflow-marketplace.json` into the existing workflow prepare/run path. This partially advances curated global skill publishing and live marketplace sync; real remote marketplace auth, remote publication, remote catalog sync, and provider-owned recipe runtimes remain future work. `npm run verify:workflow-marketplace`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover the local-only API, main/preload/mock bridge, UI, styles, roadmap evidence, and TypeScript validity.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 dependency test graph: Fork conflict review now feeds base/source/fork/draft content into the repo test graph matcher. The matcher preserves existing path-rule recommendations and adds content-derived `Import dependency graph` hints from `import`, `export ... from`, `require()`, and dynamic `import()` specifiers, `Repository dependency graph` hints from relative/aliased imports resolved to local path candidates with extension and `index.*` expansion, plus `Package script graph` hints from changed `package.json` test/typecheck/verify/build scripts. This improves merge-back test selection without live compiler services; full compiler AST diffing and reverse dependency indexing remain future work. `npm run verify:fork-worktree` and `npm run typecheck:web` cover the renderer contract, verifier evidence, and type safety.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 reverse dependency test graph: Fork conflict review now adds `Reverse dependency index` suggestions from a maintained local downstream-consumer graph. Conflicts in `src/shared/desktopApi.ts`, main IPC, preload/mock bridge code, Channels, MCP, fork runtime, workflow runtime, and generic main/shared/renderer modules can surface the typecheck and verifier commands most likely to cover downstream consumers before a resolved draft is staged. This is still a bounded rule index rather than full repository import scanning or compiler AST analysis. `npm run verify:fork-worktree` and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 document input summaries: Channels `file-input` now attempts bounded read-only text previews for workspace-local `.docx` files and simple PDF text operators when selected files are imported. The importer still rejects symlinks/out-of-workspace paths, uses no external process, does not call providers, and falls back to explicit attachment review copy when text is unavailable. This partially advances richer file/document input; live microphone capture, transcription, OCR, and full PDF layout extraction remain follow-up work. `npm run verify:channel-adapters`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist` cover the shared contract, main runtime, roadmap evidence, and checklist evidence.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 connector auth preparation: Channels now exposes `Prepare auth` for mobile, Slack, GitHub, Docs, and Calendar adapters. `desktop:channel-adapter-auth-start` creates a bounded workspace-scoped authorization preparation record with provider scopes, user code, verification URI, expiry, `authPreparedAt`, and placeholder credential state, then returns review copy without opening a browser, polling a provider, storing tokens, or enabling live sends. This advances OAuth/device/session handshakes from session-only stubs to a reviewed local preparation contract; real provider polling, token exchange, credential storage, and live connector sync/send remain follow-up work. `npm run verify:channel-adapters`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover the shared API, main/preload/mock bridge, Channels UI, styles, roadmap evidence, and TypeScript validity.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 model-aware context budget: The chat composer now derives the visible context budget from the selected model's `token_limit` / `max_tokens` metadata when present, reserves 2048 tokens for output, clamps tiny limits to a minimum preview budget, and falls back to the prior 12000-token recommendation when no model limit is known. The context preview displays whether the limit came from model metadata or fallback budget before send. This improves model-limit alignment without adding provider calls or changing gateway behavior; exact backend tokenizer parity remains follow-up work. `npm run verify:context-assembler` and `npm run typecheck:web` cover the renderer contract, UI evidence, and TypeScript validity.

Current run addendum 2026-07-08 script file inputs: Channels `file-input` now treats workspace-local PowerShell, batch, and shell-family scripts as bounded source-code static previews, including `.ps1`, `.psm1`, `.psd1`, `.bat`, `.cmd`, `.sh`, `.bash`, and `.zsh`. The importer extracts module/source/call hints, functions or batch labels, static review cues, process-boundary hints, and MIME provenance without launching PowerShell, cmd.exe, bash, or any interpreter. Live script execution, shell linting, policy signing, dependency resolution, and sandboxed dry-run remain follow-up work. `npm run verify:channel-adapters`, `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist`, and `npm run typecheck:node` cover this slice.

Current run addendum 2026-07-10 specialized PowerShell script input: Channels `file-input` now routes reviewed workspace-local `.ps1`, `.psm1`, and `.psd1` files through a specialized PowerShell script preview before generic source-code summaries. The importer reads only `MAX_POWERSHELL_SCRIPT_PREVIEW_BYTES`, caps function, parameter, module/assembly import, command, comment-help, risk-cue, and manifest-key previews with `MAX_POWERSHELL_SCRIPT_ITEM_PREVIEW`, masks secret-shaped values, and preserves `text/x-powershell` / `text/x-powershell-data` MIME provenance. It performs no PowerShell/pwsh process launch, script execution, execution-policy change, module import, remote session, credential lookup, network call, or provider send. `npm run verify:channel-adapters`, `npm run verify:channel-adapter-route-order`, `npm run verify:channel-adapter-runtime-fixtures`, `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist`, and `npm run typecheck:node` cover this slice with a runtime `runtime.ps1` golden fixture; AST-accurate parsing, PSScriptAnalyzer integration, module dependency resolution, signed-script trust validation, execution-policy validation, remoting safety analysis, and live PowerShell runtime validation remain follow-up work.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 export surface test graph: Fork conflict review now scans conflicted TypeScript/JavaScript modules for exported declarations, named/default exports, and Python modules for explicit `__all__` lists. When source/fork/draft content introduces public symbols relative to the merge base, the semantic preview adds `Export surface graph` recommendations such as node/web typechecks, UI verification, approval/chat verifiers for shared desktop API changes, and feature verifiers for recognizable channel/MCP/fork/workflow runtime paths. This improves downstream-risk coverage without filesystem scans, live compiler services, or provider calls; a full repository-wide import index and compiler-backed AST analysis remain follow-up work. `npm run verify:fork-worktree` and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 repository import index: Fork conflict review now maintains `FORK_CONFLICT_REPOSITORY_IMPORT_INDEX` in the shared analyzer. It normalizes changed paths and local import candidates into module keys, then adds `Repository import index` recommendations for known downstream importer surfaces covering shared desktop API, execution policy, channel adapters, MCP runtime, fork conflict analysis, workflow runtime, and workspace checkpoints. This reduces merge-back blind spots without scanning the filesystem at review time; generated source-derived import-index extraction and compiler-backed AST analysis remain follow-up work. `npm run verify:fork-worktree` and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 generated repository import index: Fork conflict review now consumes a static import index generated from local Windows app source metadata. `npm run generate:fork-import-index` scans `src/main`, `src/preload`, `src/renderer/src`, and `src/shared`, resolves relative plus `@shared` / `@renderer` imports, writes `src/renderer/src/components/forkConflictGeneratedImportIndex.ts`, and the analyzer emits `Generated repository import index` suggestions before maintained fallback rules. This advances generated import-index extraction without runtime filesystem scanning or provider calls; compiler AST diffing and additional non-TypeScript import sources remain follow-up work. `npm run verify:fork-worktree` and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

Current run addendum 2026-07-07 workflow retrospective prompt: Skills Square now turns completed or blocked durable workflow runs into an editable automatic workflow retrospective prompt. The prompt is prefilled with run name, status, outcome, message, step counts, and verification, then saves only after explicit user action as `source: "retrospective"` project memory. Dismiss writes nothing, and downstream skill draft creation/local install/marketplace submission still uses the existing reviewed actions. `npm run verify:project-memory`, `npm run verify:workflow-marketplace`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

Current run addendum 2026-07-08 import-kind provenance: The generated fork conflict import index now records whether each local dependency edge came from a static import, re-export, CommonJS require, or dynamic import expression. Conflict review suggestions use that provenance in the `Generated repository import index` label, which makes barrel modules and public API merge-back test suggestions clearer without adding runtime scans or provider calls. `npm run generate:fork-import-index`, `npm run verify:fork-worktree`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

Current run addendum 2026-07-08 Python import dependency graph: Fork conflict review now parses Python `import` and `from ... import ...` syntax from base/source/fork/draft previews, maps relative imports plus `drsai`, `drsai_ext`, and `drsai_ui` package roots into repository path candidates, and treats `__init__.py` as a package module key. The resulting `Repository dependency graph` and `Import dependency graph` suggestions give Python conflict reviewers more specific tests without runtime scans, provider calls, or compiler services. `npm run verify:fork-worktree` and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

Current run addendum 2026-07-08 generated Python import index: The static fork conflict import-index generator now scans `drsai`, `drsai_ext`, and `drsai_ui` Python package sources, extracts `import` / `from ... import ...` edges, resolves relative imports and package-root module keys, and emits generated downstream suggestions with `python-import` provenance. Current run addendum 2026-07-08 compiler AST export index: Fork conflict review now has a source-derived TypeScript compiler AST export-symbol index without bundling compiler services into the renderer. `scripts/generate-fork-import-index.mjs` parses TypeScript/JavaScript sources with the local TypeScript compiler API, emits `FORK_CONFLICT_GENERATED_AST_EXPORT_INDEX` with symbol names and syntax kinds, and the analyzer adds `Compiler AST export graph` suggestions for changed public symbols that match generated module surfaces. `npm run generate:fork-import-index`, `npm run verify:fork-worktree`, `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice; richer live compiler-service diagnostics and packaged merge-back smoke remain follow-up work.

Current run addendum 2026-07-08 compiler diagnostic coverage: Fork conflict review now emits a generated TypeScript compiler diagnostic coverage index. Current run addendum 2026-07-08 live draft diagnostic preflight: Fork conflict review now inspects the edited resolved draft before write-back/staging for syntax that would block compiler diagnostics, including remaining conflict markers, unmatched or unclosed delimiters, unterminated strings/templates, and unterminated block comments. The analyzer labels these as `Live draft compiler diagnostic preflight` suggestions before static compiler diagnostic coverage hints, keeping the renderer compiler-free while still directing reviewers to the relevant typecheck/verify commands. `npm run verify:fork-worktree`, `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice; full project compiler-service semantic diagnostics remain follow-up work.

Current run addendum 2026-07-08 backend-aligned context token estimate: The composer context preview now estimates attachments and workspace instructions from a backend-shaped serialized prompt that includes attachment headers, kind/path/content labels, visible browser/terminal/selection/folder body labels, fixed prompt preamble overhead, and mixed ASCII/CJK/punctuation token heuristics. This makes budget warnings closer to what the main-process chat path injects while avoiding provider tokenizer dependencies and leaving the actual send path unchanged. `npm run verify:context-assembler`, `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice; provider-exact tokenizer parity remains follow-up work.

Current run addendum 2026-07-08 packaged fork merge-back approval smoke: The packaged E2E hook now supports `OPENDRSAI_E2E_FORK_MERGE`, and `npm run verify:e2e-fork-merge` launches `release/win-unpacked/OpenDrSai.exe` with a temporary `DRSAI_HOME` after `npm run build:unpack`. The smoke first creates temporary source/worktree workspaces, creates a fork thread through the real preload bridge, queues a `fork.lifecycle` merge-back approval, verifies Approval Center pending state, rejects the approval, and confirms the fork remains `active` instead of merged or closed. It then creates a throwaway Git repository and real worktree fixture, registers both as trusted workspaces, queues and approves merge-back through the same Approval Center path, verifies the source file content, advanced HEAD, two-parent merge commit, pending cleanup metadata, and merged fork lifecycle state, then queues and approves discard cleanup and verifies closed lifecycle state, removed worktree, and merged branch deletion. A separate conflict fixture verifies approved merge-back moves to `merge_pending`, preserves the source HEAD/content, and aborts the failed Git merge cleanly. `npm run verify:fork-worktree`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and packaged `npm run verify:e2e-fork-merge` cover this slice after `npm run build:unpack`.

Current run addendum 2026-07-10 packaged fork lifecycle smoke verification: The rebuilt `release/win-unpacked` app now passes `npm run verify:e2e-fork-merge` end to end. The run exposed and fixed packaged preload API drift by adding `decideApproval` as an alias for `decidePendingApproval`, kept cleanup constrained to `DRSAI_HOME\\desktop\\fork-worktrees` by moving E2E Git worktree fixtures under the desktop-managed fork root, and normalized Windows CRLF/LF line endings in packaged smoke content assertions. `npm run verify:approval-center`, `npm run verify:fork-worktree`, `npm run typecheck:node`, `npm run typecheck:web`, `npm run build:unpack`, and `npm run verify:e2e-fork-merge` cover this slice; remaining multi-agent work is focused on fuller compiler-service semantic diagnostics and broader generated import indexing.

Current run addendum 2026-07-08 tokenizer calibration samples: Model catalog entries now accept optional bounded `tokenizer_calibration` samples containing known token counts. The budget tooltip now reports raw estimate, calibrated estimate, sample count, model limit, and output reserve. This improves provider-exact token accounting without bundling tokenizer libraries or making provider calls; collecting real provider sample packs remains follow-up work. `npm run verify:context-assembler`, `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

Current run addendum 2026-07-08 workspace tokenizer calibration packs: The model catalog now merges bounded tokenizer samples from a reviewed workspace-local `.drsai/tokenizer-calibration.json` file into gateway model metadata. Current run addendum 2026-07-08 tokenizer calibration drift signal: The context budget meter now reports calibration quality from the same reviewed sample pack data it already uses for provider-token correction. Workspaces without that opt-in keep the existing approved-but-blocked missing live provider result. Current run addendum 2026-07-22 channel credential boundary: Legacy session-placeholder configuration is removed from the public Desktop API, shared production runtime, Renderer mock, and Channels UI contract. GitHub local Git remote configuration remains read-only; GitHub Device OAuth and Slack/Google provider-token paths must complete a verified provider exchange before an adapter becomes configured. Untyped legacy `session_stub` configuration requests fail closed and do not create the channel connection store. Earlier roadmap entries describing `Configure session`, generated local authorization codes, or provider session stubs are superseded by this boundary. `npm run verify:channels --workspace opendrsai-macos-desktop`, `npm run verify:channel-adapters --workspace opendrsai-windows-desktop`, and both desktop typechecks cover the contract.

Current run addendum 2026-07-22 channel authorization recovery: The bounded Channel connection store now migrates legacy provider placeholders on read. Only an unexpired, schema-valid GitHub Device OAuth pending record with a valid `authOperationId` survives; legacy Slack/Docs/Calendar stubs, pre-verification GitHub stubs without an operation, and expired operations are atomically removed. The adapter list exposes the non-secret operation identifier so Channels can resume polling after a Renderer reload, while completion, denial, expiry, and revocation clear it. Pending authorization never satisfies GitHub import authorization; local Git metadata or a completed provider credential is required. Shared Channel/provider tests and the Windows verifier cover migration, reload recovery and fail-closed import.

Current run addendum 2026-07-22 external readiness provider parity: The user-visible External Readiness matrix now derives live/read-only semantics from each adapter's verified `authMode` instead of retaining the original snapshot-only roadmap copy. GitHub Device OAuth, Slack history/send, Docs live read/revision-bound append, and Calendar live inbound reads are recorded as implemented evidence, while gaps are limited to capabilities that remain absent. Fake-provider tests verify live and expired transitions, and static assertions reject the four superseded “live provider pending” claims.

Current run addendum 2026-07-22 mock readiness parity: Demo mode now derives GitHub, Slack, Docs, and Calendar readiness from the mutable mock adapter catalog after local configuration, OAuth completion, provider-token configuration, or revoke. Writable/read-only state follows production semantics, Calendar remains inbound-only, summary counts are recalculated on every read, and the mock evidence/gap text no longer advertises implemented live-provider paths as pending. Static contract tests bind this derivation to the mock bridge.

Current run addendum 2026-07-22 channel catalog truthfulness: The production and demo adapter catalogs now enumerate implemented GitHub Device OAuth/live comments, Slack live history/approved sends, Docs live reads/revision-bound appends, Calendar live inbound events, dedicated Mobile Pairing, and configuration-gated Voice capture. Empty local snapshot results point to the corresponding separately verified live path instead of claiming it is pending. Database connections and live log streaming remain explicitly out of scope for their snapshot-only adapters. Static tests require both the positive implemented declarations and the two honest remaining boundaries.
